*********************************************************************************
Commit: 65541ef12688a4931a95c868d8bc6561ec7b354b 
*********************************************************************************

[AMT]

  Send MAPT MAC on each reset except WARM reset
  
  [Issue Description]
  When ME region is re-flashed after MAPT MAC address is set,
  CSME loses the MAPT MAC address and BIOS does not set it again.
  This could be due to resiliency flow where CSME FW is recovered
  or due to a special manufacturing flow.
  As a result the CSME loses the MAPT functionality.
  
  [Resolution]
  Add sending MAPT MAC configuration on each reset, except
  warm reset.
  
  Package/Module:
  Features/Manageability/AmtMacPassThroughFeaturePkg
  
  [Impacted Platform]
  PantherLake
  ^^^^^^^     
  
  Hsd-es-id: 18044056375
  Change-Id: If5d8e7703e14f46f301ecb1ad95b19849f9f3870
  Original commit hash: 9532127b50a700e811f121d67d4ac052b8767b10
  
  [Changed Files]
  Features/Manageability/AmtMacPassThroughFeaturePkg/AmtMacPassThrough/AmtMacPassThrough.c
  Features/Manageability/AmtMacPassThroughFeaturePkg/AmtMacPassThrough/AmtMacPassThrough.inf
  Features/Manageability/AmtMacPassThroughFeaturePkg/Readme.md

Commit: d963060912b3a4b94a1bea8a174283d2262dd4ec 
*********************************************************************************

[ASL]

  Fix upstream RMA: Set PCIe EP VT-d to 2nd-stage vs passthrough
  
  [Issue Description]
  When SD card was not connected on respective platforms,
  system caused the IOMMU driver to define a certain PCIe endpoint
  BDF as stage-two instead of passthrough. This is because the
  SD card controller implementation for those ports were always enable.
  
  [Resolution]
  Introducing VpdPcd to control when SD card is connected to
  specific ports on specific system.
  
  Package/Module:
  BoardPkg
  
  [Impacted Platform]
  ALL
  
  Hsd-es-id: 16029406392
  Change-Id: Ib7929684967971708c4076a93d31d7e6ab49d8aa
  Original commit hash: 4fbdc9827b7a5820ee72a18e1ce8c39e7b86ae77

*********************************************************************************
Commit: 0800b6182951b67c295a1f62b9b5592487170bf2 
*********************************************************************************


  Add HECI protocol dependency in PlatformMeUpdateHookLib
  
  [Issue Description]
  Failed to get CSME FW image type with customers' BIOS,
  MeFwu->FwType() returns 0x1C error.
  ImageTypeId in ME FMP reports a fallback/default one.
  
  [Resolution]
  PlatformFmpGetMeTypeIdGuidPtr() is invoked in FMP driver EntryPoint.
  MeFwu->FwType inside this function is linked to FwUpdateLib which
  leverages HECI protocol to accomplish the required action.
  Add explicit HECI protocol dependency to ensure HECI protocol
  is installed before invoking MeFwu->FwType().
  
  Package/Module: PantherLakeBoardPkg/Features/CapsuleUpdate
                  ^^^^^^^                                    
  
  [Impacted Platform]
  All
  
  Hsd-es-id: 16029413446
  Change-Id: Ia740f500fd4d7a0153c598244bd6ffc01697dcbb
  Original commit hash: 525b3f5fba8c43fc8756c39dee130d0bd3d03df6
  
  [Changed Files]
  PantherLakeBoardPkg/Features/CapsuleUpdate/Library/PlatformMeUpdateHookLib/PlatformMeUpdateHookLib.inf

*********************************************************************************

[AMT]

  Recovering vPro TBT Dock MAPT MAC Address after CSME FW Recovery
  
  [Issue Description]
  When ME region is re-flashed after MAPT MAC address is set,
  CSME loses the MAPT MAC address and BIOS does not set it again.
  This could be due to resiliency flow where CSME FW is recovered
  or due to a special manufacturing flow.
  As a result, the CSME loses the MAPT functionality.
  
  [Resolution]
  When "HW Asset" indication is set in MBP,
        ^^                                  
  BIOS shall send the MAPT MAC address to CSME FW
  
  Package/Module:
  Features/Manageability/AmtMacPassThroughFeaturePkg/
  
  [Impacted Platform]
  ALL
  
  Hsd-es-id: 18044005129

Commit: fd467c877becabab6328777f707cd0049980611c 
*********************************************************************************

[AMT]

  Send MAPT MAC on each reset except WARM reset
  
  [Issue Description]
  When ME region is re-flashed after MAPT MAC address is set,
  CSME loses the MAPT MAC address and BIOS does not set it again.
  This could be due to resiliency flow where CSME FW is recovered
  or due to a special manufacturing flow.
  As a result the CSME loses the MAPT functionality.
  
  [Resolution]
  Add sending MAPT MAC configuration on each reset, except
  warm reset.
  
  Package/Module:
  Features/Manageability/AmtMacPassThroughFeaturePkg
  
  [Impacted Platform]
  PantherLake
  ^^^^^^^     
  
  Hsd-es-id: 18044056375
  Change-Id: If5d8e7703e14f46f301ecb1ad95b19849f9f3870
  Original commit hash: 9532127b50a700e811f121d67d4ac052b8767b10

[ASL]

  Fix upstream RMA: Set PCIe EP VT-d to 2nd-stage vs passthrough
  
  [Issue Description]
  When SD card was not connected on respective platforms,
  system caused the IOMMU driver to define a certain PCIe endpoint
  BDF as stage-two instead of passthrough. This is because the
  SD card controller implementation for those ports were always enable.
  
  [Resolution]
  Introducing VpdPcd to control when SD card is connected to
  specific ports on specific system.
  
  Package/Module:
  BoardPkg
  
  [Impacted Platform]
  ALL
  
  Hsd-es-id: 16029406392

*********************************************************************************
Commit: 8cb731f67e7033e35d47812298c8e7bdcc0304a1 
*********************************************************************************


  Add HECI protocol dependency in PlatformMeUpdateHookLib
  
  [Issue Description]
  Failed to get CSME FW image type with customers' BIOS,
  MeFwu->FwType() returns 0x1C error.
  ImageTypeId in ME FMP reports a fallback/default one.
  
  [Resolution]
  PlatformFmpGetMeTypeIdGuidPtr() is invoked in FMP driver EntryPoint.
  MeFwu->FwType inside this function is linked to FwUpdateLib which
  leverages HECI protocol to accomplish the required action.
  Add explicit HECI protocol dependency to ensure HECI protocol
  is installed before invoking MeFwu->FwType().
  
  Package/Module: PantherLakeBoardPkg/Features/CapsuleUpdate
                  ^^^^^^^                                    
  
  [Impacted Platform]
  All
  
  Hsd-es-id: 16029413446
  Change-Id: Ia740f500fd4d7a0153c598244bd6ffc01697dcbb
  Original commit hash: 525b3f5fba8c43fc8756c39dee130d0bd3d03df6

*********************************************************************************

[AMT]

  Recovering vPro TBT Dock MAPT MAC Address after CSME FW Recovery
  
  [Issue Description]
  When ME region is re-flashed after MAPT MAC address is set,
  CSME loses the MAPT MAC address and BIOS does not set it again.
  This could be due to resiliency flow where CSME FW is recovered
  or due to a special manufacturing flow.
  As a result, the CSME loses the MAPT functionality.
  
  [Resolution]
  When "HW Asset" indication is set in MBP,
        ^^                                  
  BIOS shall send the MAPT MAC address to CSME FW
  
  Package/Module:
  Features/Manageability/AmtMacPassThroughFeaturePkg/
  
  [Impacted Platform]
  ALL
  
  Hsd-es-id: 18044005129
